- **shipmates-auto-trunk**: Shipmates: Run a test-driven, trunk-based development loop — parse a goal into a task list, write a failing test first, implement the smallest change, run the repo's checks, and commit to trunk. - **shipmates-consolidate-issues**: Shipmates: Review every open issue against git history, close what's already done or stale, migrate the legacy ones, slim the backlog, and bundle the survivors into themed groups so similar work ships together efficiently. Read-only by default — it reports; changes happen only on an explicit apply. ([saman-mb/shipmates](https://github.com/saman-mb/shipmates/blob/HEAD/commands/shipmates-consolidate-issues.md) · ★6)
- **shipmates-deslop**: Shipmates: Audit a codebase for health debt — dead code, duplication, redundancy, over-engineering beyond actual need, inconsistency, bad patterns, misplaced files and folders, dependency and config rot — grade every finding by risk, then file the work as an epic with sub-issues. Never edits the codebase: it analyses, files tracked work, and stops at a human gate where the captain chooses what ships now and what waits. ([saman-mb/shipmates](https://github.com/saman-mb/shipmates/blob/HEAD/commands/shipmates-deslop.md) · ★6)
- **shipmates-document**: Shipmates: Write or refresh documentation that actually works — the technical-writer drafts it from the real code, then a fresh reader agent follows the steps against the repo and must reach the stated result. Loops until the docs are drift-free and completable. ([saman-mb/shipmates](https://github.com/saman-mb/shipmates/blob/HEAD/commands/shipmates-document.md) · ★6)
- **shipmates-fix-bug**: Shipmates: Fix a bug the honest way — reproduce it as a failing test first, root-cause it, apply the minimal fix, and prove it with the test flipping red→green while the suite stays green. Worktree-isolated, CI-gated, opens a PR. ([saman-mb/shipmates](https://github.com/saman-mb/shipmates/blob/HEAD/commands/shipmates-fix-bug.md) · ★6)
- **shipmates-harden**: Shipmates: Security-harden a surface — threat-model it, rank the findings, and re-review until every finding has a fix or an explicit accepted-risk note (and secrets/dependency scans are clean). Read-only by default — it reports; remediation happens on a branch, opt-in. ([saman-mb/shipmates](https://github.com/saman-mb/shipmates/blob/HEAD/commands/shipmates-harden.md) · ★6)
- **shipmates-migrate**: Shipmates: Run a mechanical migration across a whole codebase — discover every call site, transform each in isolation, verify per-site, and gate on a clean sweep (no old-pattern remnants) with the suite green. For API/dependency/pattern/framework migrations. ([saman-mb/shipmates](https://github.com/saman-mb/shipmates/blob/HEAD/commands/shipmates-migrate.md) · ★6)
- **shipmates-onboard**: Shipmates: Read an unfamiliar repo and write the agent-facing context file every other command depends on — conventions, commands, boundaries and the quality bar, proven by running them. Gated on a fresh agent answering the crew's real questions from the file alone. ([saman-mb/shipmates](https://github.com/saman-mb/shipmates/blob/HEAD/commands/shipmates-onboard.md) · ★6)
- **shipmates-plan-epics**: Shipmates: Turn a brief (or several) into a tracked backlog — GitHub epics + linked, labelled user stories, with a context-selected planning panel authoring and reviewing in parallel. ([saman-mb/shipmates](https://github.com/saman-mb/shipmates/blob/HEAD/commands/shipmates-plan-epics.md) · ★6)
- **shipmates-polish**: Shipmates: Iterate a visual / UI / output artifact to a specialist's sign-off — produce → critique → fix, looping until the art-director, ux-ui-designer, or product-manager is genuinely happy (or a round cap). ([saman-mb/shipmates](https://github.com/saman-mb/shipmates/blob/HEAD/commands/shipmates-polish.md) · ★6)
- **shipmates-pr-review**: Shipmates: Run the specialist acceptance board against an existing pull request the crew didn't author — classify the diff, pull the right reviewers, and return one consolidated verdict. Read-only by default: it reports, it never repairs. ([saman-mb/shipmates](https://github.com/saman-mb/shipmates/blob/HEAD/commands/shipmates-pr-review.md) · ★6)
- **shipmates-qa**: Shipmates: Walk a captain through interactive, one-step-at-a-time local QA of a PR, issue branch, or named branch — risk-targeted by default, optional blind smoke — logging findings for handoff. Guides and reports; it never repairs. ([saman-mb/shipmates](https://github.com/saman-mb/shipmates/blob/HEAD/commands/shipmates-qa.md) · ★6)
- **shipmates-refactor**: Shipmates: Change the shape of the code without changing what it does — pin current behaviour in characterization tests first, transform, then prove equivalence by those tests passing unmodified and no existing test being deleted, skipped or loosened. ([saman-mb/shipmates](https://github.com/saman-mb/shipmates/blob/HEAD/commands/shipmates-refactor.md) · ★6)
- **shipmates-release**: Shipmates: Cut a release safely — assemble the changelog from what actually merged, bump the version, gate on green CI at the tag, run an SRE pre-flight (rollback + migration safety), then tag and (opt-in) publish. ([saman-mb/shipmates](https://github.com/saman-mb/shipmates/blob/HEAD/commands/shipmates-release.md) · ★6)
- **shipmates-report-bug**: Shipmates: File a structured bug report on the Shipmates repository from a live run — harness, command, repro, expected vs observed, in the format maintainers triage. ([saman-mb/shipmates](https://github.com/saman-mb/shipmates/blob/HEAD/commands/shipmates-report-bug.md) · ★6)
- **shipmates-ship-epic**: Shipmates: Ship an epic by driving /shipmates-ship-issue over its stories in dependency-ordered waves — independent units fan out concurrently; one epic plan amortizes overhead, gate stories pause for sign-off. ([saman-mb/shipmates](https://github.com/saman-mb/shipmates/blob/HEAD/commands/shipmates-ship-epic.md) · ★6)
- **shipmates-ship-issue**: Shipmates: Take one or more GitHub issues/stories from open → reviewed PR (→ merged, opt-in) autonomously — worktree, subagent build, CI gate, specialist acceptance board, absorb-first nit disposition. ([saman-mb/shipmates](https://github.com/saman-mb/shipmates/blob/HEAD/commands/shipmates-ship-issue.md) · ★6)
- **shipmates-spike**: Shipmates: De-risk an open technical decision with a time-boxed spike — frame the question, prototype several approaches in parallel as throwaways, judge them against the project's constraints, and return a recommendation as an ADR. - **sig:add**: Capture-and-route new work — appends a stamped entry to .planning/ISSUES-INBOX.md (default; falls back to the legacy FUTURE-IDEAS.md name if that is what the repo still has) after verbatim capture, sensitive-data scrub, and atomic write. Route explicitly with --question (OPEN-QUESTIONS.md), --bug (BUGS.md), or --milestone [N] (a milestone holding section). Naked /sig:add (no args) asks one question and files the answer to the inbox. Not phase-gated. ([insightriot/signal](https://github.com/insightriot/signal/blob/HEAD/plugin/commands/add.md) · ★1)
- **sig:advise**: Review this project's own .planning/ docs for the big picture, propose 3–5 priorities — every claim carrying a citation that resolves — and ask which to work on. Records the pick in its dated advisory and offers to start it. Read-only except for that advisory. Not phase-gated. ([insightriot/signal](https://github.com/insightriot/signal/blob/HEAD/plugin/commands/advise.md) · ★1)
- **sig:build**: Build one backlog item: see the open problems beside it, size it with the person, build it, and leave the record of what was done and why in .planning/BACKLOG.md. Needs only a BACKLOG.md; no phases. ([insightriot/signal](https://github.com/insightriot/signal/blob/HEAD/plugin/commands/build.md) · ★1)
- **sig:calibrate**: Phase 0 — classify project into SKETCH / FEATURE / SPIKE / FULL and write .planning/PROFILE.md to drive downstream rigor. ([insightriot/signal](https://github.com/insightriot/signal/blob/HEAD/plugin/commands/calibrate.md) · ★1)
- **sig:checkpoint**: Manual state refresh — diffs git log against STATE.md and refreshes it. Default quick mode shows a one-line readout + diff; --context adds decision + open-question capture so a context-clear that follows can re-orient cleanly. ([insightriot/signal](https://github.com/insightriot/signal/blob/HEAD/archive/commands/checkpoint.md) · ★1)
- **sig:discuss**: DISCUSS phase — gather implementation decisions through adaptive questioning before planning. Loads idea-refine and spec-driven-development skills. ([insightriot/signal](https://github.com/insightriot/signal/blob/HEAD/plugin/commands/discuss.md) · ★1)
- **sig:docs-archive**: File finished work out of the live .planning/ root into .planning/archive/. Only units that are genuinely closed move; every other unit is reported with the reason it was declined. Dry-run by default; --apply writes. Not phase-gated. ([insightriot/signal](https://github.com/insightriot/signal/blob/HEAD/plugin/commands/docs-archive.md) · ★1)
- **sig:docs-index**: Regenerate .planning/INDEX.md — the auto-generated documentation map. Walks the corpus, renders mechanical rows (path · growth-policy), re-attaches curated notes by key, and writes only when the content changed. Idempotent; not phase-gated. ([insightriot/signal](https://github.com/insightriot/signal/blob/HEAD/plugin/commands/docs-index.md) · ★1)
- **sig:docs-migrate**: Auto-sensing doc-runtime migrate — reorganizes THIS project's .planning/ docs to the current layout (de-prose frontmatter, relocate bloated bodies, evict closed-Epic narrative, build the archive tree; and on a v2→v3 layout bump: rename FUTURE-IDEAS→ISSUES-INBOX, create BACKLOG, evict closed-milestone DECISIONS sections). Relocate-never-delete, dry-run by default, git-reversible. ([insightriot/signal](https://github.com/insightriot/signal/blob/HEAD/plugin/commands/docs-migrate.md) · ★1)
- **sig:docs-sweep**: Read-only doc-hygiene sweep over the invoking project — dead internal links, unfilled [FILL IN] stubs, INDEX/roster/version drift, a stale capture inbox, and CLAUDE.md bloat. Detect-and-report only, with one exception: on a v2 work store it confirms fixed closes whose commit is on the default branch. Touches no network. Not phase-gated. ([insightriot/signal](https://github.com/insightriot/signal/blob/HEAD/plugin/commands/docs-sweep.md) · ★1)
- **sig:doctor**: Claude Code plugin install-state diagnostician. Detects 5 documented failure modes, can generate a remediation shell script (--fix surgical, --reinstall full canonical), and reports healthy or actionable findings. macOS only first ship. ([insightriot/signal](https://github.com/insightriot/signal/blob/HEAD/archive/commands/doctor.md) · ★1)
- **sig:escalate**: Adjust tier mid-flight, UP OR DOWN. Re-runs calibration carrying current context, updates PROFILE.md (the active Epic's, if one is open), and appends to escalation_history. Use when scope or stakes grow — and equally when the work turns out smaller than the project's ceiling (de-escalation; no back-fill needed going down). ([insightriot/signal](https://github.com/insightriot/signal/blob/HEAD/plugin/commands/escalate.md) · ★1)
- **sig:execute**: EXECUTE phase — build the plan's tasks wave by wave, tests first where required, one commit each. ([insightriot/signal](https://github.com/insightriot/signal/blob/HEAD/plugin/commands/execute.md) · ★1)
- **sig:init**: Brownfield onboarding — bring Signal to an existing codebase. Scans the repo, writes .planning/LANDSCAPE.md, drafts a baseline PROJECT.md, then hands off to /sig:calibrate. ([insightriot/signal](https://github.com/insightriot/signal/blob/HEAD/plugin/commands/init.md) · ★1)
- **sig:item**: Change work items in the work store — new, triage, move, close, reopen, edit, show, list, and confirm a close by hand where git never can. Every change to an item goes through this command as an event on its record, so an item's status is always derived from its own history. Only for projects with the store on (.planning/work/WORK.md). Not phase-gated. ([insightriot/signal](https://github.com/insightriot/signal/blob/HEAD/plugin/commands/item.md) · ★1)
- **sig:new-project**: Initialize a new project with the Signal workflow. Creates .planning/ directory, PROJECT.md, and kicks off the CALIBRATE phase. ([insightriot/signal](https://github.com/insightriot/signal/blob/HEAD/plugin/commands/new-project.md) · ★1)
- **sig:permissions**: Propose an intentional permission set for this project — what Signal's flow needs, plus what this project's own stack needs — as an allowlist and a short deny list the USER installs. - **sig:plan**: PLAN phase — research, a plan in vertical slices with acceptance criteria, plan validation, and a test for every criterion. ([insightriot/signal](https://github.com/insightriot/signal/blob/HEAD/plugin/commands/plan.md) · ★1)
- **sig:resume**: Resume work on a Signal project. Reads PROFILE.md + STATE.md + the current phase's artifact, prints a re-orientation, and asks if you're ready to continue. ([insightriot/signal](https://github.com/insightriot/signal/blob/HEAD/plugin/commands/resume.md) · ★1)
- **sig:review**: REVIEW phase — review the built change for quality, security, performance and simplicity, have fresh-context agents read it, fix what they find, and record a verdict. ([insightriot/signal](https://github.com/insightriot/signal/blob/HEAD/plugin/commands/review.md) · ★1)
- **sig:ship**: SHIP phase — check the change is ready, open the pull request, record the phase and close the work in the project's records, in one commit. ([insightriot/signal](https://github.com/insightriot/signal/blob/HEAD/plugin/commands/ship.md) · ★1)
- **sig:status**: Read-only project inspection — synthesizes PROFILE.md + STATE.md (and OPEN-QUESTIONS.md if present) into a one-screen status report with tier-aware next-action guidance. ([insightriot/signal](https://github.com/insightriot/signal/blob/HEAD/plugin/commands/status.md) · ★1)
- **sig:update**: Report the installed Signal version against what's available, show the CHANGELOG entries in between, update on confirmation, and state plainly that a restart is required. Read-only until you confirm; fail-open offline. Not phase-gated. ([insightriot/signal](https://github.com/insightriot/signal/blob/HEAD/archive/commands/update.md) · ★1)
- **sig:verify**: VERIFY phase — check the built work against its acceptance criteria and requirements, run the suite and build, and write a report that states its totals. ([insightriot/signal](https://github.com/insightriot/signal/blob/HEAD/plugin/commands/verify.md) · ★1)
